What’s the most cost efficient way w/r/t ski passes to ski 25 days in Aspen

**A:**

→ **Aspen Premier Pass** is the most direct route for 25 days, providing unlimited access across all four mountains (**Aspen Mountain, Snowmass, Aspen Highlands, and Buttermilk**) with early-season pricing listed at **$3,699**

→ **Ikon Pass** covers your first **7 days** combined across the resort complex, but because multi-day add-on packs cap out at 7 days (via the Flex Pass), relying on Ikon leaves you short 18 days

→ **Daily lift tickets** run around **$279+ per day**, meaning buying 18 additional days at window rates would cost upwards of **$5,000+** on top of your Ikon base, making single-day purchases uneconomical for a 25-day total

→ **Chamber pass programs** (through the Aspen Chamber Resort Association) offer significant discounts on local passes if you qualify through local business ownership or employment verification

**Q:** what is the least valuable company by market cap on the NYSE or nasdaq

**A:**

→ There is no single, permanent company holding the absolute bottom spot on the NYSE or Nasdaq because nano-caps and distressed shells constantly rotate through bankruptcies, liquidations, and delistings.

→ Major exchanges enforce strict continued-listing standards, including minimum market value requirements and a **$1.00** minimum bid price rule, which triggers deficiency notices and pushes companies falling below those thresholds out to the OTC pink sheets.

→ The companies sitting at the absolute bottom of the exchange rosters typically trade as distressed micro-caps or incoming delistings with market capitalizations hovering between **$500,000 and a few million dollars** right before their trading is suspended.

**Q:** what are the psych wards in Boston

**A:**

→ **McLean Hospital** in Belmont, the nation's premier psychiatric teaching hospital, affiliated with Harvard Medical School and Mass General Brigham, offering comprehensive inpatient, residential, and specialized behavioral care.

→ **Massachusetts General Hospital (MGH)** in Boston, operates acute inpatient psychiatric units, specialized specialty tracks, and emergency behavioral evaluation as part of the Mass General Brigham network.

→ **Boston Medical Center (BMC)** in Boston, provides acute inpatient psychiatric stabilization, emergency mental health triage, and integrated community behavioral health units.

→ **Cambridge Health Alliance (CHA)** across Cambridge and Everett campuses, runs dedicated short-term inpatient psychiatric units for acute stabilization in coordination with local emergency departments.

→ **Arbour Hospital** in Boston (Jamaica Plain), provides inpatient and outpatient psychiatric stabilization and behavioral health treatment programs for adults.
